## §Carry

Agglomerated packets. Every row cites its banked id ⟨witness records⟩; full mechanism at the bank. **Bold locks are binding verbatim.**

> **KF-AV-28 · STANDING SUPERSESSION RIDER (KF.W7's defining lock) — carried, not cited.** Banked at `kf-AnimationVisualizer.md:35`, `kf-PlaybackRibbon.md:36`, `kf-SequenceScrubber.md:36`: KF.W7's S-9 evaluation (KF-AV-28) may supersede any behavioral cure on a NO-WAVE-OWNER row banked under an evaluated surface; a per-surface SWAP verdict discharges the bespoke rows banked under that surface with the component. Rows in this spec governed by the rider are spent CONDITIONALLY: no cure on a governed row lands before KF.W7's verdict for its surface, or the executing seat accepts the throwaway explicitly; at execution the seat STATES which governed rows a SWAP verdict has discharged BEFORE spending any cure (KF-W7 G15's discharge discipline).

### W6-A · THE TABS DELETE (S-1, census-corrected: DELETE, not replace)

⟨kf-ChannelControls · kf-KfPillTabs · kf-KeyframesStringControls RR-D · kf-AnimationControlsGroup RR-D⟩ — 26 A-family rows discharge with one delete, under two locks.
